FIXES, both append-logged in the frontier log with the same real output: the two chain blocks restructured to exit-code truth (sys.exit(0 if ok else 1); `&& echo PASS || { echo FAIL; FAIL=1; }`; verdict texts, thresholds and every number unchanged) and the parity boundary corrected to `forming = last_label >= today` with a dated comment (the intended semantics taken from the stage 7 snapshot instrument's own recorded convention, "the card always refers to the last completed W-FRI week").
